# Household hh_s12 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, dining, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Elena** (resident_1): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.67); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.86); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.55); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: baking, cycling, friends. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Priya** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.88); average timing (jitter scale 0.93); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.56); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: guitar, music, yoga. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Elena.

## Day 18 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `sick_day:resident_1` (Elena): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Elena has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Priya is running late all day (0.97): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.
